Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..20)
find y: int(0..5)
find z: int(0..6)

such that

(x / (y / z)) = 10

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#order_1: bool
  find x#order_10: bool
  find x#order_11: bool
  find x#order_12: bool
  find x#order_13: bool
  find x#order_14: bool
  find x#order_15: bool
  find x#order_16: bool
  find x#order_2: bool
  find x#order_3: bool
  find x#order_4: bool
  find x#order_5: bool
  find x#order_6: bool
  find x#order_7: bool
  find x#order_8: bool
  find x#order_9: bool

   ~~> div_to_bubble ([("Bubble", 6000)])
{SafeDiv(SATInt(Order, [y#order_1,y#order_2,y#order_3,y#order_4,y#order_5,y#order_6;int(1..)] [0, 5]), SATInt(Order, [z#order_1,z#order_2,z#order_3,z#order_4,z#order_5,z#order_6,z#order_7;int(1..)] [0, 6])) @ SATInt(Order, [z#order_1,z#order_2,z#order_3,z#order_4,z#order_5,z#order_6,z#order_7;int(1..)] [0, 6]) != 0}

   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])

new variables:
  findAux __6: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__6 <-> integer-weighted(1*z#order_2, 1*z#order_3, 1*z#order_4, 1*z#order_5, 1*z#order_6, 1*z#order_7) NotEqual 0) using None with [PbTermGroup { start: 0, end: 6, structure: Chain }]

--
